<script type="text/javascript">
// <![CDATA[

        // TODO: Ookla has a prebid timeout of 2000, we may want to configure admanager to set a variable timeout to match this

        var ads;
        var googletag = googletag || {};
        googletag.cmd = googletag.cmd || [];

        function loadDfp(callback) {
          var gads = document.createElement('script');
          gads.async = true;
          gads.type = 'text/javascript';
          var useSSL = 'https:' == document.location.protocol;
          gads.src = (useSSL ? 'https:' : 'http:') + '//www.googletagservices.com/tag/js/gpt.js';
          var node = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
          node.parentNode.insertBefore(gads, node);
          callback();
        }

        function loadAdManager(displayAds){
            let options = {
              'mode': 'singleRequest',
              'disableInitialLoad': true,
              'PREBID_TIMEOUT': 2000,
              'prebidConfig': {
                enableSendAllBids: false,
                pubcid: {
                    enable: true,
                    expInterval: 105120
                },
                userSync: {
                    enabledBidders: ['rubicon','appnexus','ix','openx'],
                    iframeEnabled: true
                },
                consentManagement: {
                    cmpApi: 'iab',
                    allowAuctionWithoutConsent: true
                }
              }
            };
            // TODO: Add Slot Level targetting.
            let targets = {'slotTargets': {
              'company': '',
              'categories': '',
            }};

            (function (w) {
              w.URLSearchParams = w.URLSearchParams || function (searchString) {
                  var self = this;
                  self.searchString = searchString;
                  self.get = function (name) {
                      var results = new RegExp('[\?&]' + name + '=([^&#]*)').exec(self.searchString);
                      if (results == null) {
                          return null;
                      }
                      else {
                          return decodeURI(results[1]) || 0;
                      }
                  };
              }
            })(window)

            ads = (window.pbjs.version) ? new window.OOKLA.globals.AdManager.Prebid(adZones, targets, options) : new window.OOKLA.globals.AdManager.Dfp(adZones, targets, options);
            if (displayAds) {
              displayAds();
            }
        }

        function displayAdUnits(){
            if(banner_size){ads.display('home', 'ad-leaderboard')};
            if(rectangle_size){ads.display('home', 'mpu-ad-top')};
            if(rectangle_size_bottom){ads.display('home', 'mpu-ad-bottom')};
            if(inline_size){ads.display('home', 'ad-inline-top')};
            if(inline_size_second){ads.display('home', 'ad-inline-bottom')};
            if(mobileleaderboard_size){ads.display('home', 'ad-mobileleaderboard')};
        }

        function loadSocial(){
            if (typeof loadFacebook === "function"){loadFacebook()};
            if (typeof loadTwitter === "function"){loadTwitter()};
            if (typeof loadDisqus === "function"){loadDisqus()};
            if (typeof loadVk === "function"){loadVk()};
        }

        function loadFacebook()
        {
            if (banner_size && banner_size[0] && banner_size[0][0]) {
                $('body').addClass('size-' + banner_size[0][0]);
                (function(d, s, id) {
                    var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];
                    if (d.getElementById(id)) return;
                    js = d.createElement(s); js.id = id;
                    js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/nl_NL/sdk.js#xfbml=1&version=v3.0&appId=116781521799556";
                    fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fjs);
                }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));
            }
        }

// ]]>
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
// <![CDATA[
        !function(){
            var cmpRan = false;
            window.zdconsent = window.zdconsent || {'cmd': [] };
            window.zdconsent.cmd.push(function(window) {
                cmpRan = true;
            })

            if (window.zdconsent.inited && (!window.zdconsent.gdprApplies || window.zdconsent.consentGiven)){
                loadDfp(function () {
                    loadAdManager();
                })
                displayAdUnits();
            }
            else{
                window.zdconsent = window.zdconsent || {'cmd': [] };
                window.zdconsent.cmd.push(function(window) {
                    loadDfp(function () {
                        loadAdManager();
                    })
                    displayAdUnits();
                })

                var adDelay = adDelay || 2000;

                setTimeout(function(){
                    if(!cmpRan){
                        loadDfp(function () {
                            loadAdManager();
                        });
                        displayAdUnits();
                    }
                }, adDelay);
            }
        }();

// ]]>
</script>

                    $('#countries-li').on('click', function(e) {
                      $('#countries-dropdown').toggle('collapse');
                      e.stopPropagation();
                    })

                    $(document).on('click', function(e) {
                      if (e.target.classList.contains('form-control')) {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        return;
                      }

                      $('#countries-dropdown').hide();
                      $('.navbar-collapse').removeClass('show');
                      $('.search-form .form-group').css('width', '1rem');
                      $('.search-form .form-group').css('border', '1px solid #111');
                      $('input[name=q]').blur();
                    });

                    $(document).on('click', '#dropdownSearchButton', function(e) {
                      $('.navbar-collapse').removeClass('show');
                      $('#countries-dropdown').hide();

                      if ($('.search-form .form-group').css('width') !== '16px') {
                        // close search
                        $('.search-form .form-group').css('width', '16px');
                        $('.search-form .form-group').css('border', '1px solid #111');
                        $('input[name=q]').blur();
                        $('.search-form').blur();
                        $('.toggler-search-button').blur();
                      } else {
                        $('.search-form .form-group').css('width', '13rem');
                        $('.search-form .form-group').css('border', '1px solid #333');
                        $('input[name=q]:visible:first').val('').focus();
                      }
                      e.stopPropagation();
                    });

<script type="text/javascript">
// <![CDATA[
    // lazy load any images, and render the sparklines
    function initImages() {
      $("img.lazy").lazyload({
        threshold: 200,
        effect : "fadeIn"
      });
      $('.sparkline').each(function(i, o){
        chart = $(o);
        values = chart.data('values');

        if(!values || values.length == 0) {
          values = [0];
        }

        // check for any full zero companies, if so, render a straight line
        max = values.reduce(function(a, b) {
            return Math.max(a, b);
        });
        if(max == 0) {
          // only add the element if it doesn't exist yet
          if(!chart.hasClass('zero')) {
            chart.parent().append('<div class="sparkline zero"></div>')
            chart.remove();
          }
        } else {
          available_width = $($(o).prev()).width();
          $(o).attr('width', available_width);
          sparkline.sparkline(document.querySelector('#sparkline-' + chart.data('id')), values);
        }
      })
    }
    var column_width = 999999;

    $(function() {
      initImages();

      column_width = $('.company-index:first').width();

      // when we resize the window, re-render all sparklines and logo's
      // but only if the actual size changed
      $(window).resize(function(){
        if($('.company-index:first').width() != column_width) {
          column_width = $('.company-index:first').width();
          initImages();
        }
      });

      // When clicking on "show more", load all companies, and render all
      // sparklines and logo's lazy
      $('#showmore .btn').on('click', function(e){
        ga("global.send", "event", "index", "show-more");
        $('#showmore .loading').show();
        $('#showmore a').hide();

        $('#more-companies-container').load('/more-companies/ #more-index-companies', function(data){
          // Sometimes, because of timings, we end up with double companies
          // filter those and remove the second element from the DOM
          $('.sparkline[id]').each(function(){
            var ids = $('[id="'+this.id+'"]');
            if(ids.length>1 && ids[0]==this)
              $(ids[1]).closest('.company-index').remove();
          });

          initImages();
          $('#showmore .btn').hide(); // hide all buttons
        });
        event.preventDefault();
        return false;
      })
    });

// ]]>
</script>
